PID regional buses · Benešovsko (okres Benešov) · Monday 2 Nov 2026

PID Benešovsko

Every PID bus line of okres Benešov (licences 20xxxx) and the Prague and other lines whose outer end lies here, set as Simutrans lines with the owner's regional method: each line read over the whole day at its core points, a repeating interval, destination and shift variants on their own longer intervals (at most 480 min), timetables at the terminals worked back from the knots, every afternoon stop on a line, buses sized from the pattern. Okres Benešov with its Prague lines (332, 337, 339 from Budějovická; 382, 383 from Háje; 406, 426, 401, 408 from Roztyly) and the lines from the Jihočeský kraj and Vysočina that reach it (401, 408, 453, 455, 456, 457, 459, 846, 865, 866, 867). Every terminus is a loop: each typed value is the real minute − 1. Outstation lines (339, 382, 755: the buses sleep at the far end) are timed both ways at the inbound rate; their extra departures towards the far end are listed, as the owner set on 25 Oct 2026. Where the real timetable sends the arriving bus straight back (426 and 406 at Roztyly, 453 at Tábor and Mladá Vožice, 794 at Vlašim, 840, 841, MHD Benešov 3, 848 Zdiměřice), the leg has a 0-minute wait so the bus count follows the real turn; this assumes the fork's automatic Leave up to lets a bus that arrives on the real minute leave although the loop rule typed it 1 min earlier (an engine question, still to confirm). Vlašim, Terminál is a timed pulse at :50, when 426 leaves for Prague and 790, 455, 842 and others arrive (their 0-minute chains to 426 are this connection, not through buses). Through-running seen in the timetable, to confirm on the live map of 2 Nov: 337's Benešov – Nespeky shorts run on as 651 to Pyšely (all of them); 408 and 459 buses go on as 453 at Tábor (9 of 16, 5 of 11); the Sulice, Želivec shorts of 339 follow 335 arrivals (7 of 7); MHD Benešov 4 is interlined with 1 and 2. Pooling (not applied): 426's Prague trips and its Benešov shorts turn into each other at Vlašim in about 10 minutes; 791's two ends alternate at Benešov (3 buses as one circuit instead of 4); 752 Týnec's shorts fit in the Jílové buses' 55-minute layover at Benešov (752 and 752 Týnec, 5 buses, about 3 pooled); 453 Vlašim runs with 453 at Tábor and Mladá Vožice (4 buses, 3 pooled). X390 (Rabyně – Slapy) replaces line 390's closed section over the Slapy dam until 31 Dec 2027 and is modelled as it runs. Alternative to 651 Nespeky, if the real working should show: one circuit 651 Benešov every 120 (Benešov 15:29 → Nespeky as 337, on at 15:48 → Pyšely as 651, Pyšely 14:55 → Nespeky, on at 15:02 → Benešov as 337), 1 bus, the same as now. Owner, 2 Nov 2026: places whose only afternoon service is PID Haló (on demand) get a Simutrans line from their fixed trips, at most every 480 (here 848 Zdiměřice, which also runs Haló 887's Novotinky loop).

How to read the line cards

Typing the timetable. Values go into the Scheduling tab of the terminal entry, with the calendar on: Departure every, Offset, Also at (at most 7). Offsets count from 01:00 and every terminal is set as a loop, so each value is (real departure − 61) mod interval; the real clock minute is under every value. A row with Every 0 has no timetable: the bus leaves on arrival.

Core points and knots. Each line was read over the whole operating day (5:00–23:00) at its core points: terminals, knots where variants split, shared hubs. A trip counts at the time it passes a core point, wherever it starts. Where a slot is defined at a knot, the terminal departure is the knot time minus the running time, and the knot gets no timetable.

Variants. Each destination, path or shift variant is its own Simutrans line on a longer interval (the save game's Ex4). Shift lines repeat every 480 (8 h), the longest interval allowed. Trips more than 3 min off every slot are off the pattern, in orange.

Buses needed. One bus followed round the line's own slots with the real running times and at least 3 min at each terminal. Real buses swap lines at the terminals, so per-line totals are higher than what the operators run; pooling lines is the owner's choice and is not done.

In the game. Each card compares the save's line (export of 30 Oct 2026) with the modelled stops. The save's buses are shown as a check; the sheet is built from the real timetable and the buses seen running, so a game line may need buses added or moved.

To check

Choices made here that you may want to flip, each with its alternative; then the Simutrans lines whose model window has a slot without a real departure, or a real departure more than 3 min off.

How this was built

Scope. PID GTFS downloaded Sun 1 Nov 2026 (feed 1-14 Nov). Service day Mon 2 Nov 2026 (the first live-map poll day), a normal school weekday (28 Oct was a holiday and 29-30 Oct autumn school holidays; regional lines run many school-day-only trips). The Středočeský kraj licenses each district's lines with its own prefix (20xxxx = okres Benešov); a line licensed by Prague or by another kraj is on the sheet of the okres of its outer end (the first stop, coming in from the far end, in a Středočeský municipality), or, with no Prague end, of the okres most of its stops lie in. 68 lines: 63 modelled here, 0 on the Prague bus sheet, 5 listed with the reason.

Method. pipeline/bus-schedule-method.md (v4): whole-day reading at core points, repeating interval, Ex4 variants, timing at terminals worked back from the knots, no daily lines (at most every 480), every afternoon stop on a line, buses from the pattern, every line checked by hand (method-check file in the pipeline folder).

Buses. PID live map (mapa.pid.cz getData.php), polled every minute 12:00-20:00 (every 3 min 5:00-12:00 and 20:00-23:00) on Mon 2 and Tue 3 Nov 2026 by scripts/01_live_poll.py; each bus once per line through getVehicleWindow.php (operator, type, course). A bus fills one Simutrans line it ran in that line's window, by matching, across all twelve oblast sheets; buses on the Prague bus sheet are not used here.

Sources: PID GTFS, PID live map, the save-game export of 30 Oct 2026 (engine session).
